Its output matches the original exactly, and the simulation scales to about 16–32 threads on this 256-thread machine.\n\n**What changed** (only `unstructured/unstructured.cpp` and `unstructured/CMakeLists.txt`; `common/` is untouched):\n\n- **Simulation loop (`runSimulation`)**: one `#pragma omp parallel` region covers all iterations. Each step is an `omp for schedule(static)` over elements, and the buffer swap is an `omp single`. The implicit barriers order the reads and writes, so the fork/join cost is paid once instead of per iteration. The loop body is unchanged, and each element still reads only the current buffer and writes its own slot in the swap buffer.\n- **Mesh build (`buildSquare2D`)**: the element-initialization loop and the connectivity loop are parallel. Each writer touches only its own element, so this is race-free.\n- **Hash (`computeHash`)**: a `reduction(^:hash)`. XOR is associative and commutative, so the result is identical.\n- **`CMakeLists.txt`**: `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX` linked to the target. The `-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra` flags and the `m` link are kept, and the executable name is unchanged.\n\n**What I deliberately left serial:** `validateResults`. Parallelizing its floating-point sums would change summation order, which can alter the printed `Energy sum` and `Flux sum` digits and the validation semantics. It runs outside the timed region, so it doesn't affect the benchmark numbers.\n\n**Verification:**\n- Built the original and new versions in `/tmp` (outside the project) and compared stdout, excluding the timing lines, plus exit codes. Configurations covered `-v -r` at 512, 64, 300, and 1 elements per side, and `-i 0`. Every run matched at 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 64, and 256 threads.\n- Build has zero warnings with `-Wall -Wextra`, and `libgomp` is linked.\n\n**Timing** (2048×2048 grid, 50 iterations, computation time only):\n\n| Threads | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| ms | 1971 | 1120 | 746 | 411 | 209 | 175 | 182 | 418 |\n\nThe original takes 1957 ms. Scaling flattens past about 32 threads because the loop is memory-bandwidth bound: `ElementStatic` is 144 bytes, and four of its eight connection slots are unused. Shrinking that struct, for example to a structure-of-arrays layout, would be the next lever for performance, but it is a data-layout change beyond the parallelization you asked for, so I didn't make it.\n\nThe project is not a git repository, so I did not commit anything."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":559,"cache_read_input_tokens":54825,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":559},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5ce65524-81e7-454f-a5b4-8cf2f3fab483","uuid":"b207f09a-0b98-4615-9a72-00bb047e53c9","timestamp":"2026-10-13T08:12:57.725Z","request_id":"req_011CfyuB3t4P822nk2nngeUT"}
